- **fable-audit-verifier**: Blind adversarial verifier for one fable-audit candidate finding. Receives the claim and evidence locations — never the reviewer's rationale — and tries to refute it. Read-only; returns a verdict object only. ([ish-dev-piqc/PIQC-dev-v1](https://github.com/ish-dev-piqc/PIQC-dev-v1/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fable-audit-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **fable-builder**: 오케스트레이터(메인 세션)가 tasks.json 또는 워크플로 인자로 배정한 구현 태스크를 격리 worktree에서 코드+테스트로 완성한다. Agent Teams·Workflow의 개발(Build) 슬롯 전용. "구현해줘", "빌드 태스크", "모듈 병렬 개발" 신호일 때 사용. ([claude-code-expert/carve-harness](https://github.com/claude-code-expert/carve-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fable-builder.md) · ★16)
- **fable-captain**: Council lead contract. Fable 5 orchestrates: decomposes the task, frames one research question and runs the /deep-research dynamic workflow, then spawns three Opus 5 teammates — opus-synthesizer, opus-clarity, opus-janitor — and reconciles their live SendMessage traffic into one final answer. This is the LEAD's contract, adopted by the main session; it is never spawned as a teammate. ([ANcpLua/ancplua-claude-plugins](https://github.com/ANcpLua/ancplua-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/council/agents/fable-captain.md) · ★6)

- **fable-consultant**: Weighs in on one hard question on behalf of a caller who has already done the legwork and wants a second read — on a design, a diagnosis, a plan, or a choice they keep re-opening. Returns an honest read, the hardest push-back against the caller's own position, and the next thing worth checking. Reads the code directly when a claim needs checking. Advisory only. ([pt-hieu/skills](https://github.com/pt-hieu/skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/brian/agents/fable-consultant.md) · ★1)

- **fable-dev**: Fable 级开发工程代理(Opus 升级预设)。任何实质性开发任务——实现/修复/重构/部署/排障——都用它当主力工程师。它以 Fable 5 的工程纪律运行:证据定义完成、根因修复、范围手术刀、回合必须闭环。需要 Fable 水准产出而当前模型是 Opus 系列时,这是默认选择。 ([Jaydenlk/HR](https://github.com/Jaydenlk/HR/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fable-dev.md) · ★4)
- **fable-developer**: Fable 5-backed implementation engineer for the hardest tasks — ones that resist atomization (frontier complexity). Executes one task per dispatch from an ai-crew team-lead. Reads the exact spec and plan line ranges cited in the prompt plus the required skills; follows TDD and incremental-implementation; returns a one-line PASS or FAIL summary. Cannot dispatch subagents — strict 1-level dispatch. ([Goktug/ai-crew](https://github.com/Goktug/ai-crew/blob/HEAD/agents/fable-developer.md) · ★6)
- **fable-doc-writer**: 빌드 결과·리서치 산출물을 근거로 문서를 작성/갱신한다. README·가이드·API 문서·아키텍처 문서 담당. Agent Teams·Workflow의 문서(Document) 슬롯 전용. 코드가 확정된 뒤 호출한다. "문서화해줘", "가이드 써줘", "README 갱신" 신호일 때 사용. ([claude-code-expert/carve-harness](https://github.com/claude-code-expert/carve-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fable-doc-writer.md) · ★16)

- **fable-gate-reviewer**: Renders a product verdict on a bounded decision packet, for a coordinator that needs one without returning to the director. - **fable-high**: Generic worker pinned to model Fable 5.1 at effort high. Use only when explicitly dispatching per the superpowers-dispatch policy — never auto-select. ([UmutEmreOnder/superpowers-dispatch](https://github.com/UmutEmreOnder/superpowers-dispatch/blob/HEAD/agents/fable-high.md) · ★1)
- **fable-judge**: Judgement lane — the most expensive model, for planning and final verdict/synthesis only. Fable 5.1 at max effort. - **fable-low**: General-purpose worker running Fable 5 at low reasoning effort. Use for subtasks that need frontier-model judgment with fast turnaround — non-trivial code changes with a clear spec, careful summarization or review of large material, tricky-but-bounded fixes. Fable at low effort still outperforms prior models at high effort. Prefer opus-low for purely mechanical or routine work (Fable costs more per token). - **fable-max**: Maximally capable, do-anything generalist running Fable 5 at maximum effort. Delegate the hardest, most open-ended, ambiguous, cross-cutting, or multi-step tasks here — deep research, architecture and design, end-to-end implementation, debugging, refactoring, data work, writing, analysis, and anything that needs the strongest model with the deepest reasoning. '라이선스 검사', 'leaked 전문 들어갔는지', 'attribution 확인', '소스 라이선스 재확인', '배포/push 해도 되는지', 'FL15 승인', 그리고 '다시 검사', '재실행', '치유 후 재검증', '이전 license 결과 기반 보완' 요청을 모두 처리한다. - **fablelayer-conductor**: FableLayer 개발 하네스 총괄 오케스트레이터. `/fablelayer`, "FableLayer 만들어", "FableLayer 하네스", "Fable 레이어 빌드", "절차 레이어 주입", "ValueOptimizer 적용", "벤치 재현", "플러그인/CLI/어댑터 패키징" 요청에 발동한다. 후속 작업 키워드도 받는다: "재실행", "resume", "status", "수정", "보완", "이전 결과 기반", "이어서", "audit", "publish". 모드(new/layer/bench/package/audit/status/resume/publish)를 판정하고, runs/&lt;run_id&gt;/RUN_MANIFEST.json을 단일 갱신하며, phase 통과는 gates/verify_fablelayer.sh의 exit code로만 판정한다(자가보고 금지). 하위 에이전트를 Agent 도구로 디스패치하고 최종 보고한다. FL1, PromptCore, core/ 산출물, 프롬프트 구조 추출, 패턴 ledger, 버전 디렉토리 요청이면 이 에이전트를 호출한다. Reports findings; does not silently rewrite. ([jamesdbloom/learn-ice-hockey](https://github.com/jamesdbloom/learn-ice-hockey/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/facts-reviewer.md))
- **factual-auditor**: Auditor fáctico. Verifica autores con iniciales correctas, artículos legales con número específico, fechas, referencias normativas y datos cuantitativos. Coteja con notas.md. NO valida APA (eso lo hace bibliography-auditor) ni word count (quality-auditor). ([devwspito/libroforge](https://github.com/devwspito/libroforge/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/factual-auditor.md))
- **factual-knowledge-extractor**: Extracts data models, entities, classes, and relationships from codebase to understand WHAT exists ([jingnanzhou/fellow](https://github.com/jingnanzhou/fellow/blob/HEAD/agents/factual-knowledge-extractor.md) · ★4)
- **factual-knowledge-extractor-v2-ast**: AST-enhanced extractor for data models, entities, and relationships with 80%+ token reduction ([jingnanzhou/fellow](https://github.com/jingnanzhou/fellow/blob/HEAD/agents/factual-knowledge-extractor-v2-ast.md) · ★4)
- **factual-reviewer**: READ-ONLY ANALYSIS AGENT: Verifies implementation matches planning documents and ensures all planned features are actually implemented. Catches discrepancies between plans and reality. NEVER modifies code - only analyzes. ([barnabasJ/claude](https://github.com/barnabasJ/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/essentials/agents/factual-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **factual-reviewer**: Read-only review lens for technical accuracy — checks that a diff does what the PR, linked issue, and docs claim, and flags contradictions with documented contracts. Use in the ship review stage. ([brezzy1337/claude-t3-devkit](https://github.com/brezzy1337/claude-t3-devkit/blob/HEAD/claude-t3-devkit/agents/factual-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **factual-reviewer**: Read-only review lens for technical accuracy in a Unity / C# diff — checks that the change does what the PR, linked issue, and docs claim (including platform claims such as WebGL or dedicated-server support), and flags contradictions with documented contracts, stale XML docs or tooltips, and tests that assert the wrong thing. - **falsification-analyst**: The single adversarial verification gate (SPEC §6b). Treats each finding as a hypothesis under test, decomposes it into atomic claims, generates disconfirming queries, runs web-only adversarial search, assigns an ordinal verdict (falsified | weakened | survived | inconclusive), and writes that verdict through scripts/falsify.sh into extensions.harness.verification. Then applies remediation: falsified → quarantine, weakened → downgrade one level, survived/inconclusive → annotate only. Enforces the one-round rule. Spawned by the orchestrator (Phase 2) or invoked standalone via /falsify.
